# Adversarial review — sa-monitor v1 HC event wire (NEW additive feature)

Context: additive lane. classify(NewsItem)->Optional[HCEvent] (precision-biased keyword classifier for FDA approval/CRL/clearance + Phase 1/2/3 trial readouts met/missed/topline). _emit_hc_events in halt_monitor.run() consumes the news stream sa-monitor already polls, attributes via self-identified tickers in the PR ∩ covered universe, dedups via persisted hc_events_emitted set (post-then-mark, delivery-gated by slack_mode, mirrors the shipped _emit_followups). Alerts to #street-account. Existing halt/resume/follow-up paths unchanged.

THE #1 RISK IS CLASSIFIER FALSE POSITIVES — a wrong 'Phase 3 met' or 'FDA approval' alert destroys trust. Hunt hard for headlines that WRONGLY classify (or misreport direction). Specifically verify these attack vectors and report any that fire when they should not:
- Regulatory negative-gating ASYMMETRY: _classify_regulatory gates _APPROVAL with _APPROVAL_NEGATIVE but does NOT gate _CLEARANCE. Does 'submits 510(k) for FDA clearance' / 'seeking FDA clearance' / 'files for FDA clearance' false-fire as a clearance event? (I believe YES — confirm.)
- Future/pending trial headlines the _TRIAL_NEGATIVE misses: 'announces topline Phase 3 data expected in Q4' (is 'expected in' caught?), 'Phase 3 readout on track for 2H26', etc.
- Direction faithfulness: any headline where met/missed is attached to a NON-primary thing yet reads as met/missed primary; secondary-endpoint-only headlines.
- Competitor/partner cross-reference PRs ('comments on FDA approval of X').
- Also check the emit path: post-then-mark correctness, delivery gating (dry-run never marks), dedup key collisions (news_id|symbol|event_type), any crash on missing/empty fields, Block Kit context uses elements[].

+"""HC event-wire layer (v1 — PR-wire consumer).
+
+Consumes the PRN/BW/GlobeNewswire NewsItems that halt_monitor already fetches
+each news poll, classifies each into an HC event type (clinical-trial topline
+readout / FDA approval / CRL / medtech clearance), and — via the emit path in
+halt_monitor — fires an SA-faithful #street-account alert when the issuer's
+self-identified ticker is in the covered universe.
+
+Design of record: research/sa_monitor_hc_event_wire_design_2026-07-13.md.
+
+- `types.HCEvent`  — the classified-event dataclass.
+- `classify.classify(item) -> Optional[HCEvent]` — precision-biased keyword
+  classifier anchored to the locked headlines in template-library.md §15/§16/§19.
+
+The universe filter, per-ticker attribution, dedup, and delivery gating live in
+halt_monitor._emit_hc_events — this package only decides *what kind of event*
+a headline is (or None), never *whether to fire*.
+"""
diff --git a/src/events/classify.py b/src/events/classify.py

@@ -0,0 +1,208 @@
+"""Precision-biased keyword classifier: NewsItem -> Optional[HCEvent].
+
+Design of record: research/sa_monitor_hc_event_wire_design_2026-07-13.md §3.
+Rules are anchored to the LOCKED real headlines in template-library.md
+§15 (Phase-N primary MISSED), §16 (FDA approval), §19 (Phase-N primary MET).
+
+Philosophy — "miss over misfire" (design §7): a wrong "Phase 3 met" or "FDA
+approval" alert costs more trust than a missed one. Every rule requires the
+headline to CLEARLY match a completed action. Ambiguous, future-tense,
+procedural, or presentation headlines return None.
+
+classify() only proposes an event *type/direction/phase*; it never decides
+whether to fire. The universe filter + attribution + dedup live in
+halt_monitor._emit_hc_events. classify() returns an HCEvent with symbol="".
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import re
+from typing import Optional
+
+from ..news.types import NewsItem
+from .types import (
+    CLEARANCE,
+    CRL,
+    DIR_MET,
+    DIR_MISSED,
+    DIR_NA,
+    FDA_APPROVAL,
+    TRIAL_READOUT,
+    HCEvent,
+)
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Trial readout
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+# "Phase 1/2/3" (also "Phase 2B", "Phase 1/2") — capture the leading digit.
+_PHASE = re.compile(r"\bphase\s*([123])", re.I)
+
+# Result attached to the PRIMARY (or co-/dual primary) endpoint. Tying the
+# verb to "primary" (rather than matching "met"/"missed" anywhere) keeps the
+# reported DIRECTION faithful, which is the harmful failure mode to avoid.
+_MET_PRIMARY = re.compile(
+    r"\b(met|meets|achieved|achieves|hit|reached)\b"
+    r"(?:\s+(?:its|the|all|both|all\s+of\s+its))?"
+    r"(?:\s+(?:co-|dual|two|three|multiple))?"
+    r"\s+(?:co-)?primary\b",
+    re.I,
+)
+# Anchored to primary/endpoint so "did not meet enrollment target / guidance"
+# (with a stray Phase in the title) can't read as a failed readout.
+_MISSED_PRIMARY = re.compile(
+    r"\b(did\s+not\s+meet|does\s+not\s+meet|failed\s+to\s+meet|fails\s+to\s+meet"
+    r"|missed|misses|did\s+not\s+achieve|does\s+not\s+achieve)\b"
+    r"(?:\s+(?:its|the|both|all|all\s+of\s+its))?"
+    r"(?:\s+(?:co-|dual))?"
+    r"\s+(?:primary|co-primary|endpoint)",
+    re.I,
+)
+# A topline-data announcement with no explicit met/missed in the headline is
+# still a genuine readout crossing the wire — fired at direction n/a.
+_TOPLINE = re.compile(r"\btop-?line\b", re.I)
+
+# Future / procedural / presentation headlines that mention a Phase but are NOT
+# a readout. If ANY of these match, the trial-readout path returns None. This
+# is the primary defense for the negative test set.
+_TRIAL_NEGATIVE = re.compile(
+    r"\bpresent"          # present / presents / presented / presentation / presenting
+    r"|\bto\s+report\b|\bwill\s+report\b"
+    r"|\bto\s+announce\b|\bto\s+host\b|\bto\s+provide\b|\bto\s+release\b"
+    r"|\bto\s+webcast\b|\bwebcast\b|\bconference\s+call\b|\binvestor\s+day\b"
+    r"|\bposter\b|\bat\s+the\s+\w+.*(?:conference|congress|meeting|symposium)"
+    r"|\binitiat"          # initiate / initiation / initiates
+    r"|\bcommenc"          # commence / commences / commencement
+    r"|\bfirst[\s-]patient\b|\bdosing\s+of\b|\bdoses\s+(?:the\s+)?first\b"
+    r"|\bfirst\s+patient\s+dosed\b"
+    r"|\benroll"           # enroll / enrolls / enrolled / enrollment
+    r"|\btrial\s+design\b|\bstudy\s+design\b"
+    r"|\bto\s+begin\b|\bplans?\s+to\b|\bexpects?\s+to\b|\banticipat"
+    r"|\bon\s+track\b|\bdesign(?:s|ed)?\s+to\s+evaluate\b"
+    r"|\bcompletes?\s+enrollment\b",
+    re.I,
+)
+
+
+def _classify_trial(title: str, item: NewsItem) -> Optional[HCEvent]:
+    m_phase = _PHASE.search(title)
+    if not m_phase:
+        return None
+    if _TRIAL_NEGATIVE.search(title):
+        return None  # future / procedural / presentation → not a readout
+
+    missed = _MISSED_PRIMARY.search(title)
+    met = _MET_PRIMARY.search(title)
+    topline = _TOPLINE.search(title)
+
+    if missed:
+        direction = DIR_MISSED
+    elif met:
+        direction = DIR_MET
+    elif topline:
+        direction = DIR_NA
+    else:
+        return None  # Phase present but no result grammar → not a readout
+
+    return _build(item, TRIAL_READOUT, direction, phase=m_phase.group(1))
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# FDA regulatory actions
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+# Action-verb-anchored approval grammar. Deliberately does NOT match bare
+# "FDA approval" so pending/procedural headlines ("PDUFA date for FDA approval
+# decision", "seeking FDA approval") don't fire.
+_APPROVAL = re.compile(
+    r"\bfda\s+approves\b"
+    r"|\bfda\s+approved\b"
+    r"|\bfda\s+approval\s+of\b"
+    r"|\breceive[sd]?\s+fda\s+approval\b"
+    r"|\bgranted\s+fda\s+approval\b"
+    r"|\bwins?\s+fda\s+approval\b"
+    r"|\bsecure[sd]?\s+fda\s+approval\b"
+    r"|\bgains?\s+fda\s+approval\b"
+    r"|\bfda\s+grants?\s+(?:full\s+|accelerated\s+|traditional\s+|marketing\s+)?approval\b"
+    r"|\bfda\s+has\s+granted\s+approval\b"
+    r"|\bapproved\s+by\s+the\s+fda\b",
+    re.I,
+)
+# Pending / procedural regulatory milestones that are NOT an approval. Any hit
+# suppresses the approval path (belt-and-suspenders atop the verb-anchored regex).
+_APPROVAL_NEGATIVE = re.compile(
+    r"\baccept(?:s|ed)?\b|\bpriority\s+review\b|\bfast\s+track\b"
+    r"|\bbreakthrough\s+therapy\b|\borphan\s+drug\b|\brare\s+pediatric\b"
+    r"|\badvisory\s+committee\b|\badcomm\b|\bpdufa\b"
+    r"|\bresubmi|\bseeking\b|\bawait|\bapplication\s+for\b"
+    r"|\bfiles?\s+for\b|\bsubmit|\bsubmission\b|\bto\s+request\b"
+    r"|\bcomplete\s+response\s+letter\b|\bpre-?market\s+notification\b",
+    re.I,
+)
+
+_CRL = re.compile(r"\bcomplete\s+response\s+letter\b|\bcrl\b", re.I)
+
+# Medtech clearance. Requires a completed clearance action — bare adjective
+# "FDA-cleared" (e.g. "operates an FDA-cleared facility") must NOT match.
+_CLEARANCE = re.compile(
+    r"\bfda\s+clears\b"
+    r"|\bfda\s+clearance\b"
+    r"|\breceive[sd]?\s+fda\s+clearance\b"
+    r"|\b510\(k\)\s+clearance\b"
+    r"|\bgranted\s+510\(k\)\b"
+    r"|\bde\s+novo\s+(?:clearance|authorization|grant)\b"
+    r"|\bpma\s+approval\b"
+    r"|\bfda\s+grants?\s+(?:de\s+novo|510)\b",
+    re.I,
+)
+
+
+def _classify_regulatory(title: str, item: NewsItem) -> Optional[HCEvent]:
+    # CRL first — a Complete Response Letter is unambiguous and distinct from
+    # an approval; it must never be mis-read as one.
+    if _CRL.search(title):
+        return _build(item, CRL, DIR_NA)
+    if _APPROVAL.search(title) and not _APPROVAL_NEGATIVE.search(title):
+        return _build(item, FDA_APPROVAL, DIR_NA)
+    if _CLEARANCE.search(title):
+        return _build(item, CLEARANCE, DIR_NA)
+    return None
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Public entry point
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+
+def _build(item: NewsItem, event_type: str, direction: str,
+           *, phase: str = "") -> HCEvent:
+    return HCEvent(
+        news_id=item.news_id,
+        symbol="",  # attribution decided by the emit path (tickers ∩ universe)
+        event_type=event_type,
+        direction=direction,
+        headline=item.title,
+        source=item.source,
+        url=item.url,
+        published_at=item.published_at,
+        phase=phase,
+        confidence="high",
+        raw_industries=tuple(item.industries),
+    )
+
+
+def classify(item: NewsItem) -> Optional[HCEvent]:
+    """Classify a NewsItem into an HCEvent, or None if it isn't one.
+
+    Precision-biased: returns None on any ambiguity. Regulatory actions
+    (approval / CRL / clearance) are checked before trial readouts — an
+    approval PR carries no Phase grammar, so the two families don't collide.
+    """
+    title = item.title or ""
+    if not title.strip():
+        return None
+
+    reg = _classify_regulatory(title, item)
+    if reg is not None:
+        return reg
+
+    return _classify_trial(title, item)
diff --git a/src/events/types.py b/src/events/types.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..82baeae
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/events/types.py
@@ -0,0 +1,44 @@
+"""HCEvent — a classified health-care event from the PR wire.
+
+A pure dataclass with no upward dependencies (mirrors news.types.NewsItem):
+classify() builds one from a NewsItem, and halt_monitor._emit_hc_events stamps
+the covered `symbol` per-attribution before rendering.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+
+# Event-type constants — the four families v1 fires (design §1).
+TRIAL_READOUT = "trial_readout"
+FDA_APPROVAL = "fda_approval"
+CRL = "crl"
+CLEARANCE = "clearance"
+
+# Direction values for trial readouts.
+DIR_MET = "met"
+DIR_MISSED = "missed"
+DIR_NA = "n/a"
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class HCEvent:
+    """One classified HC event.
+
+    classify() returns this with `symbol=""` (attribution is decided by the emit
+    path from the item's already-extracted tickers ∩ universe); the emit path
+    then produces a per-symbol copy via dataclasses.replace before rendering.
+
+    Dedup key (built in the emit path): f"{news_id}|{symbol}|{event_type}".
+    """
+
+    news_id: str          # == NewsItem.url (dedup + reference)
+    symbol: str           # covered ticker this event is attributed to ("" until emit)
+    event_type: str       # TRIAL_READOUT | FDA_APPROVAL | CRL | CLEARANCE
+    direction: str        # DIR_MET | DIR_MISSED | DIR_NA
+    headline: str         # NewsItem.title, verbatim (SA reproduces issuer language)
+    source: str           # "prnewswire" | "businesswire" | "globenewswire"
+    url: str
+    published_at: str     # ISO-8601 UTC
+    phase: str = ""       # "1" | "2" | "3" | "" (trial readouts only)
+    confidence: str = "high"  # high (PR-wire self-identified ticker) | medium (resolver, session 2)
+    raw_industries: tuple[str, ...] = ()

     halts_filtered_non_emit_code: int = 0
     halts_enriched_with_note: int = 0
@@ -337,6 +340,116 @@ def _emit_followups(
                             note_context=f"Follow-up: {item.title}")
 
 
+def _emit_hc_events(
+    news_items: list[NewsItem],
+    universe: Universe,
+    tracker: HaltTracker,
+    log_path: Optional[Path],
+    *,
+    stats: RunStats,
+    slack_mode: str = "off",
+    slack_webhook_url: Optional[str] = None,
+) -> None:
+    """Detect + emit HC event-wire alerts (FDA actions + clinical readouts).
+
+    v1 CONSUMES the PR-wire NewsItems already fetched this poll: classify each,
+    and — for items whose issuer-embedded tickers are in the covered universe —
+    post an SA-faithful alert (design of record §4).
+
+    Attribution is HIGH-confidence by construction: the ticker was written into
+    the issuer's own release and extracted by the news parsers, so there is no
+    name-matching / misattribution risk (the openFDA sponsor→ticker resolver is
+    deferred to session 2).
+
+    Dedup key: f"{news_id}|{symbol}|{event_type}" in tracker.hc_events_emitted —
+    one alert per (press release, covered ticker, event type), ever. Post-then-
+    mark, identical gating to _emit_followups:
+      off      → stdout emit is the delivery → mark
+      live     → mark only on a successful post
+      dry-run  → render only, never post, never mark (re-testable)
+    """
+    for item in news_items:
+        ev = classify(item)
+        if ev is None:
+            continue
+        # v1 universe filter = issuer-extracted tickers ∩ covered Universe.
+        # (session 2 adds the sponsor→ticker resolver fallback here.)
+        covered = [t for t in item.tickers if universe.get(t)]
+        if not covered:
+            continue
+        for symbol in covered:
+            dedup_key = f"{item.news_id}|{symbol}|{ev.event_type}"
+            if dedup_key in tracker.hc_events_emitted:
+                continue  # one alert per (PR, ticker, type), ever
+            meta = universe.get(symbol)
+            event = replace(ev, symbol=symbol)
+
+            rendered = render_hc_event(
+                event, sector=(meta.sector if meta else ""),
+                subsector=(meta.subsector if meta else ""),
+            )
+            print()
+            print("=" * 60)
+            print(rendered)
+            print("=" * 60)
+            sys.stdout.flush()
+
+            # dry-run: render only — never post, never mark (re-testable).
+            if slack_mode == "dry-run":
+                try:
+                    slack.post_hc_event(event, meta,
+                                        webhook_url=slack_webhook_url, dry_run=True)
+                except Exception as exc:
+                    log.error("hc-event dry-run render failed for %s: %s",
+                              symbol, exc)
+                continue
+
+            posted = True
+            if slack_mode == "live":
+                try:
+                    slack.post_hc_event(event, meta,
+                                        webhook_url=slack_webhook_url, dry_run=False)
+                    stats.slack_posts_succeeded += 1
+                except Exception as exc:
+                    posted = False
+                    stats.slack_posts_failed += 1
+                    log.error("slack hc-event post failed for %s: %s", symbol, exc)
+
+            # off: stdout emit is the delivery → mark. live: mark only on post ok.
+            if posted:
+                tracker.hc_events_emitted.add(dedup_key)
+                stats.hc_events_emitted += 1
+                log.info("hc-event emitted for %s [%s/%s]: %s",
+                         symbol, event.event_type, event.direction, event.headline)
+                if log_path:
+                    _append_hc_log(log_path, event, meta)
+
+
+def _append_hc_log(log_path: Path, event, meta) -> None:
+    """Structured JSONL audit record for a delivered HC event (mirrors
+    _append_log; HCEvent has a different shape than HaltEvent so it gets its
+    own writer rather than being coerced)."""
+    log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    record = {
+        "ts_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
+        "kind": "hc_event",
+        "emitted": True,
+        "symbol": event.symbol,
+        "event_type": event.event_type,
+        "direction": event.direction,
+        "phase": event.phase,
+        "headline": event.headline,
+        "source": event.source,
+        "url": event.url,
+        "published_at": event.published_at,
+        "confidence": event.confidence,
+        "sector": meta.sector if meta else None,
+        "subsector": meta.subsector if meta else None,
+    }
+    with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+        f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
+
+
 def _append_log(log_path: Path, kind: str, event: HaltEvent, meta,
                 *, emitted: bool, note_context: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
     log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
@@ -375,6 +488,7 @@ def _post_health_heartbeat(stats: RunStats, slack_mode: str,
         f"halts={stats.halts_emitted}",
         f"resumes={stats.resumes_emitted}",
         f"followups={stats.followups_emitted}",
+        f"hc_events={stats.hc_events_emitted}",
         f"slack_ok/fail={stats.slack_posts_succeeded}/{stats.slack_posts_failed}",
         f"fetch_errors={stats.fetch_errors}",
     ]
@@ -420,6 +534,7 @@ def run(
     analyst_days_calendar_path: Optional[Path] = None,

 )
 
@@ -182,6 +194,69 @@ def build_followup_blocks(halt: HaltEvent, item: NewsItem,
     }
 
 
+def _hc_event_emoji(event: HCEvent) -> str:
+    """Event-type emoji per design §5. Trial readouts split on direction so a
+    'met' and a 'missed' are visually distinct at a glance."""
+    if event.event_type == FDA_APPROVAL:
+        return ":pill:"
+    if event.event_type == CRL:
+        return ":x:"
+    if event.event_type == CLEARANCE:
+        return ":heavy_check_mark:"
+    if event.event_type == TRIAL_READOUT:
+        if event.direction == DIR_MISSED:
+            return ":small_red_triangle_down:"
+        if event.direction == DIR_MET:
+            return ":test_tube:"
+        return ":microscope:"  # topline, direction TBD
+    return ":pill:"
+
+
+def build_hc_event_blocks(event: HCEvent, meta: Optional[TickerMeta]) -> dict:
+    """Block Kit for an HC event-wire alert — an FDA action or clinical readout
+    on a covered name (design §5, templates §15/§16/§19). Single-section mrkdwn,
+    mirroring build_followup_blocks. Headline is the issuer's verbatim title;
+    the fact-dense efficacy body is a deferred LLM enhancement."""
+    symbol = event.symbol
+    headline = hc_event_headline(event.headline)
+    time_hhmm, date_short = hc_event_when(event.published_at)
+
+    header = f"{_hc_event_emoji(event)} *SA:* `{symbol}` — {headline}"
+
+    when = f"`{time_hhmm} ET {date_short}`" if time_hhmm else ""
+    detail_bits = [when] if when else []
+    if meta and meta.sector:
+        sector_str = meta.sector
+        if meta.subsector:
+            sector_str += f" / {meta.subsector}"
+        detail_bits.append(f"Sector: {sector_str}")
+    detail = "  ·  ".join(detail_bits)
+
+    label = _NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS.get(event.source, event.source)
+    source_line = f"_source: {label} press release"
+    if event.url:
+        source_line += f" · {event.url}"
+    source_line += "_"
+
+    lines = [header]
+    if detail:
+        lines.append(detail)
+    if meta and is_biotech(meta.subsector):
+        lines.append(biotech_triage_cta(symbol))
+    lines.append(source_line)
+    text_block = "\n".join(lines)
+    fallback = f"SA: {symbol} — {headline}"
+    return {
+        "blocks": [
+            {
+                "type": "section",
+                "text": {"type": "mrkdwn", "text": text_block[:SLACK_SECTION_TEXT_LIMIT]},
+            }
+        ],
+        "text": fallback,
+    }
+
+
 def build_dm_blocks(message: str, *, level: str = "warning") -> dict:
     emoji = {"ok": ":white_check_mark:", "warning": ":warning:", "error": ":x:"}.get(
         level, ":warning:"
@@ -258,6 +333,18 @@ def post_followup(halt: HaltEvent, item: NewsItem, meta: Optional[TickerMeta], *
     return payload
 
 
+def post_hc_event(event: HCEvent, meta: Optional[TickerMeta], *,
+                  webhook_url: Optional[str] = None,
+                  dry_run: bool = False) -> Optional[dict]:
+    payload = build_hc_event_blocks(event, meta)
+    if dry_run:
+        log.info("slack[dry-run] hc_event payload for %s:\n%s", event.symbol,
+                 json.dumps(payload, indent=2))
+        return payload
+    post_payload(payload, webhook_url=webhook_url)
+    return payload
+
+
 def post_dm(message: str, *, level: str = "warning",
             webhook_url: Optional[str] = None,
             dry_run: bool = False) -> Optional[dict]:

diff --git a/src/template.py b/src/template.py
index 3c22d2e..44cc508 100644
--- a/src/template.py
+++ b/src/template.py
@@ -208,6 +208,63 @@ def render_followup(halt: HaltEvent, item: NewsItem, *, sector: str = "",
     return "\n".join(parts)
 
 
+def hc_event_when(published_at: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
+    """(time_hhmm, date_short) in ET for an HC event, from its PR publish time.
+
+    Falls back to ("", "") if the timestamp is missing/malformed — the caller
+    then simply omits the stamp rather than printing garbage.
+    """
+    et = _iso_utc_to_et(published_at)
+    if et is None:
+        return ("", "")
+    return (f"{et.hour:02d}:{et.minute:02d}",
+            f"{et.month}/{et.day:02d}/{et.year % 100:02d}")
+
+
+def hc_event_headline(headline: str) -> str:
+    """The event headline, verbatim from the issuer, capped for readability.
+
+    SA reproduces issuer language; v1 delivers the issuer's own title (the
+    fact-dense §15/§19 efficacy body is a deferred LLM enhancement).
+    """
+    headline = (headline or "").strip()
+    if len(headline) > _FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT:
+        headline = headline[:_FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"
+    return headline
+
+
+def render_hc_event(event, *, sector: str = "", subsector: str = "") -> str:
+    """Render an HC event-wire alert to plain text (stdout / --slack off).
+
+    event is an events.types.HCEvent. Mirrors render_followup's shape:
+
+        HH:MM ET M/DD/YY [StreetAccount] TICKER {verbatim issuer headline}
+        Sector: {sector} / {subsector}
+        [biotech triage CTA if biotech]
+        Source: {Wire} press release · {url}
+    """
+    time_hhmm, date_short = hc_event_when(event.published_at)
+    headline = hc_event_headline(event.headline)
+
+    when = f"{time_hhmm} ET {date_short} ".lstrip() if time_hhmm else ""
+    header = f"{when}[StreetAccount] {event.symbol} {headline}"
+
+    parts = [header]
+    if sector:
+        sector_line = f"Sector: {sector}"
+        if subsector:
+            sector_line += f" / {subsector}"
+        parts.append(sector_line)
+    if is_biotech(subsector):
+        parts.append(biotech_triage_cta(event.symbol))
+    label = _NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS.get(event.source, event.source)
+    source_line = f"Source: {label} press release"
+    if event.url:
+        source_line += f" · {event.url}"
+    parts.append(source_line)
+    return "\n".join(parts)
+
+
 def render_resume(event: HaltEvent, *, sector: str = "", subsector: str = "",
                   name_override: str = "") -> str:
     """Render a resume notification — sa-monitor variant.
diff --git a/tests/test_hc_events.py b/tests/test_hc_events.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..ca6177a
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tests/test_hc_events.py
@@ -0,0 +1,334 @@
+"""Tests for the HC event-wire lane — classify() + _emit_hc_events.
+
+Two halves:
+
+1. classify() precision — a POSITIVE set anchored to the locked real headlines
+   in template-library.md §15/§16/§19, and a NEGATIVE set of headlines that
+   must NOT fire (presentations, trial initiation/enrollment, earnings/
+   financing/personnel PRs, pending FDA milestones, boilerplate "FDA-cleared").
+   A wrong "Phase 3 met" / "FDA approval" alert is worse than a miss, so the
+   NEGATIVE set is the load-bearing guard.
+
+2. _emit_hc_events lifecycle — detection, covered-only filter, dedup, and the
+   slack-mode delivery gate (off marks, live marks only on post success,
+   dry-run marks nothing), mirroring tests/test_followup.py.
+"""
+from datetime import datetime, timezone
+from unittest.mock import patch
+
+import pytest
+
+from src import state
+from src.coverage import TickerMeta
+from src.dedup import HaltTracker
+from src.events.classify import classify
+from src.events.types import (
+    CLEARANCE,
+    CRL,
+    DIR_MET,
+    DIR_MISSED,
+    DIR_NA,
+    FDA_APPROVAL,
+    TRIAL_READOUT,
+)
+from src.halt_monitor import RunStats, _emit_hc_events
+from src.news.types import NewsItem
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Fixtures / helpers
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+class StubUniverse:
+    """Minimal Universe stand-in — _emit_hc_events only calls .get()."""
+
+    def __init__(self, mapping):
+        self._m = {k.upper(): v for k, v in mapping.items()}
+
+    def get(self, symbol):
+        return self._m.get(symbol.upper())
+
+
+def _meta(symbol="VRDN", subsector="Biotech"):
+    return TickerMeta(symbol=symbol, name=f"{symbol} Inc",
+                      sector="Biopharma", subsector=subsector)
+
+
+def _item(title, *, tickers=("VRDN",), source="prnewswire",
+          url=None, pub="2026-05-05T11:05:00+00:00"):
+    return NewsItem(
+        source=source, title=title, body="body text",
+        url=url or f"https://prn.test/{abs(hash(title)) % 10**8}",
+        published_at=pub, tickers=tickers,
+    )
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 1a. classify() — POSITIVE set (anchored to §15 / §16 / §19)
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+POSITIVE = [
+    # (title, event_type, direction, phase)
+    # §16 FDA approval — issuer-press-release variant (ARVN 2026-05-01 real capture)
+    ("Arvinas issues press release on FDA approval of VEPPANU (vepdegestrant) "
+     "for the treatment of ESR1M, ER+/HER2- advanced breast cancer",
+     FDA_APPROVAL, DIR_NA, ""),
+    # §16 FDA approval — headline variant
+    ("FDA approves Veppanu for ESR1M, ER+/HER2- advanced breast cancer",
+     FDA_APPROVAL, DIR_NA, ""),
+    ("Acme Pharma Receives FDA Approval for Acmezumab in metastatic melanoma",
+     FDA_APPROVAL, DIR_NA, ""),
+    ("FDA grants accelerated approval of Novadrug for rare genetic disorder",
+     FDA_APPROVAL, DIR_NA, ""),
+    # §19 trial readout — MET (VRDN 2026-05-05 real capture)
+    ("Viridian reports Phase 3 REVEAL-2 trial of elegrobart in chronic thyroid "
+     "eye disease met its primary endpoint",
+     TRIAL_READOUT, DIR_MET, "3"),
+    # §19 met dual primary
+    ("Cytokinetics reports ACACIA-HCM Phase 3 met dual primary endpoints",
+     TRIAL_READOUT, DIR_MET, "3"),
+    ("Newco achieves primary endpoint in Phase 2 study of newzumab",
+     TRIAL_READOUT, DIR_MET, "2"),
+    # §15 trial readout — MISSED (DAZALS / CEDAR real phrasings)
+    ("Corcept reports Phase 2 DAZALS study of dazucorilant does not meet "
+     "primary endpoint",
+     TRIAL_READOUT, DIR_MISSED, "2"),
+    ("Bigco reports Phase 2B CEDAR study of drugx did not meet its primary or "
+     "secondary efficacy endpoints",
+     TRIAL_READOUT, DIR_MISSED, "2"),
+    ("Smallco announces Phase 3 pivotal trial failed to meet primary endpoint",
+     TRIAL_READOUT, DIR_MISSED, "3"),
+    # topline, direction TBD (still a genuine readout crossing)
+    ("Genco announces topline results from Phase 3 SUNRISE trial of gencept",
+     TRIAL_READOUT, DIR_NA, "3"),
+    # CRL
+    ("Regenco receives Complete Response Letter from FDA for regzumab",
+     CRL, DIR_NA, ""),
+    # medtech clearance
+    ("Deviceco receives FDA clearance for its AI-powered cardiac monitor",
+     CLEARANCE, DIR_NA, ""),
+    ("Deviceco announces FDA 510(k) clearance of its next-gen catheter",
+     CLEARANCE, DIR_NA, ""),
+]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("title,etype,direction,phase", POSITIVE)
+def test_classify_positive(title, etype, direction, phase):
+    ev = classify(_item(title))
+    assert ev is not None, f"expected an event, got None for: {title}"
+    assert ev.event_type == etype
+    assert ev.direction == direction
+    assert ev.phase == phase
+    assert ev.headline == title          # verbatim
+    assert ev.symbol == ""               # attribution decided in the emit path
+    assert ev.confidence == "high"
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 1b. classify() — NEGATIVE set (must ALL return None)
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+NEGATIVE = [
+    # presentations / future data disclosure (not a readout)
+    "Acme to present Phase 3 REVEAL data at the ASCO 2026 annual conference",
+    "Acme will present detailed Phase 2 results at an upcoming medical congress",
+    "Acme presents Phase 3 topline data in a late-breaking poster presentation",
+    "Acme to present topline Phase 2 data next week",
+    # trial lifecycle, not a readout
+    "Acme announces initiation of Phase 3 pivotal trial of acmezumab",
+    "Acme doses first patient in Phase 3 study of acmezumab",
+    "Acme completes enrollment in Phase 3 ELEVATE trial",
+    "Acme to host conference call to discuss Phase 3 trial design",
+    "Acme enrolls first patient in Phase 2 basket trial",
+    # non-clinical PRs (no readout, no approval)
+    "Acme reports first quarter 2026 financial results",
+    "Acme announces $150 million underwritten public offering",
+    "Acme appoints Jane Doe as Chief Executive Officer",
+    "Acme to participate in the Phase Capital healthcare investor conference",
+    # pending / procedural FDA milestones (NOT an approval)
+    "FDA accepts Acme's New Drug Application for acmezumab",
+    "FDA grants Priority Review to Acme's application for acmezumab",
+    "FDA grants Fast Track designation to acmezumab",
+    "FDA grants Breakthrough Therapy designation for acmezumab",
+    "Acme submits Biologics License Application to FDA seeking approval of acmezumab",
+    "Acme resubmits application for FDA approval of acmezumab following prior review",
+    # boilerplate adjective — must NOT read as a clearance event
+    "Acme opens new FDA-cleared manufacturing facility in North Carolina",
+    # ambiguous secondary-only readout — precision says skip
+    "Acme reports Phase 2 study met key secondary endpoint",
+]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("title", NEGATIVE)
+def test_classify_negative(title):
+    assert classify(_item(title)) is None, f"MISFIRE: {title!r} should not classify"
+
+
+def test_classify_blank_title_is_none():
+    assert classify(_item("   ")) is None
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 2. _emit_hc_events — lifecycle / gating
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def _run_emit(news_items, universe, tracker, *, slack_mode="off"):
+    stats = RunStats(started_at="t")
+    _emit_hc_events(news_items, universe, tracker, None,
+                    stats=stats, slack_mode=slack_mode)
+    return stats
+
+
+def test_covered_readout_emits_and_renders(capsys):
+    item = _item("Viridian reports Phase 3 REVEAL-2 trial of elegrobart in "
+                 "chronic thyroid eye disease met its primary endpoint",
+                 tickers=("VRDN",))
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta("VRDN")})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    stats = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker)
+    assert stats.hc_events_emitted == 1
+    key = f"{item.news_id}|VRDN|{TRIAL_READOUT}"
+    assert key in tracker.hc_events_emitted
+    out = capsys.readouterr().out
+    assert "VRDN" in out
+    assert "met its primary endpoint" in out
+    assert "[StreetAccount]" in out
+
+
+def test_negative_item_emits_nothing(capsys):
+    item = _item("Acme to present Phase 3 REVEAL data at the ASCO 2026 "
+                 "annual conference", tickers=("VRDN",))
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta("VRDN")})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    stats = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker)
+    assert stats.hc_events_emitted == 0
+    assert tracker.hc_events_emitted == set()
+    assert capsys.readouterr().out.strip() == ""
+
+
+def test_uncovered_ticker_never_fires():
+    item = _item("FDA approves Zzzdrug for advanced disease", tickers=("ZZZZ",))
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta("VRDN")})  # ZZZZ not covered
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    stats = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker)
+    assert stats.hc_events_emitted == 0
+    assert tracker.hc_events_emitted == set()
+
+
+def test_item_with_no_tickers_never_fires():
+    item = _item("FDA approves Somedrug for advanced disease", tickers=())
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta("VRDN")})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    stats = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker)
+    assert stats.hc_events_emitted == 0
+
+
+def test_partner_ticker_covered_fires_for_partner():
+    # Small biotech's approval names its covered large-pharma partner — both
+    # tickers land in item.tickers; the covered one fires.
+    item = _item("Arvinas issues press release on FDA approval of VEPPANU for "
+                 "the treatment of ER+/HER2- advanced breast cancer",
+                 tickers=("ARVN", "PFE"))
+    universe = StubUniverse({"PFE": _meta("PFE", subsector="Pharma")})  # only PFE covered
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    stats = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker)
+    assert stats.hc_events_emitted == 1
+    assert f"{item.news_id}|PFE|{FDA_APPROVAL}" in tracker.hc_events_emitted
+
+
+def test_both_covered_tickers_each_fire_once():
+    item = _item("Arvinas issues press release on FDA approval of VEPPANU",
+                 tickers=("ARVN", "PFE"))
+    universe = StubUniverse({"ARVN": _meta("ARVN"), "PFE": _meta("PFE")})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    stats = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker)
+    assert stats.hc_events_emitted == 2
+    assert f"{item.news_id}|ARVN|{FDA_APPROVAL}" in tracker.hc_events_emitted
+    assert f"{item.news_id}|PFE|{FDA_APPROVAL}" in tracker.hc_events_emitted
+
+
+def test_dedup_one_alert_per_pr_ticker_type_ever():
+    item = _item("FDA approves Veppanu for advanced breast cancer", tickers=("VRDN",))
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta("VRDN")})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    first = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker)
+    second = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker)  # same item re-fetched
+    assert first.hc_events_emitted == 1
+    assert second.hc_events_emitted == 0
+    assert len(tracker.hc_events_emitted) == 1
+
+
+def test_dry_run_renders_but_marks_nothing(capsys):
+    item = _item("FDA approves Veppanu for advanced breast cancer", tickers=("VRDN",))
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta("VRDN")})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    stats = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker, slack_mode="dry-run")
+    assert stats.hc_events_emitted == 0          # nothing delivered
+    assert tracker.hc_events_emitted == set()    # re-testable — no mark
+    assert "VRDN" in capsys.readouterr().out     # but it DID render
+
+
+def test_live_post_failure_does_not_mark():
+    item = _item("FDA approves Veppanu for advanced breast cancer", tickers=("VRDN",))
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta("VRDN")})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    with patch("src.slack.post_hc_event", side_effect=RuntimeError("slack down")):
+        stats = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker, slack_mode="live")
+    assert stats.hc_events_emitted == 0
+    assert stats.slack_posts_failed == 1
+    assert tracker.hc_events_emitted == set()   # post failed → not marked → retries next poll
+
+
+def test_live_post_success_marks():
+    item = _item("FDA approves Veppanu for advanced breast cancer", tickers=("VRDN",))
+    universe = StubUniverse({"VRDN": _meta("VRDN")})
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+
+    with patch("src.slack.post_hc_event", return_value={"ok": True}) as m:
+        stats = _run_emit([item], universe, tracker, slack_mode="live")
+    assert m.called
+    assert stats.hc_events_emitted == 1
+    assert f"{item.news_id}|VRDN|{FDA_APPROVAL}" in tracker.hc_events_emitted
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# 3. dedup / state round-trip for hc_events_emitted
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
+
+def test_reset_clears_hc_events():
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    tracker.hc_events_emitted.add("u|VRDN|fda_approval")
+    tracker.reset()
+    assert tracker.hc_events_emitted == set()
+
+
+def test_hc_events_emitted_round_trip(tmp_path):
+    tracker = HaltTracker()
+    tracker.hc_events_emitted.add("https://prn.test/1|VRDN|trial_readout")
+    tracker.hc_events_emitted.add("https://prn.test/2|ARVN|fda_approval")
+    state.save(tracker, state_dir=tmp_path, day_key="2026-05-05")
+
+    fresh = state.load(state_dir=tmp_path, day_key="2026-05-05")
+    assert fresh.hc_events_emitted == {
+        "https://prn.test/1|VRDN|trial_readout",
+        "https://prn.test/2|ARVN|fda_approval",
+    }
+
+
+def test_old_state_without_hc_field_rehydrates_empty(tmp_path):
+    path = tmp_path / "dedup_state_2026-05-05.json"
+    path.write_text(
+        '{"schema_version": 1, "trading_day_et": "2026-05-05", '
+        '"halts": [], "resumes_emitted": []}'
+    )
+    fresh = state.load(state_dir=tmp_path, day_key="2026-05-05")
+    assert fresh.hc_events_emitted == set()

   197	def _hc_event_emoji(event: HCEvent) -> str:
   198	    """Event-type emoji per design §5. Trial readouts split on direction so a
   199	    'met' and a 'missed' are visually distinct at a glance."""
   200	    if event.event_type == FDA_APPROVAL:

   202	    if event.event_type == CRL:

   204	    if event.event_type == CLEARANCE:

   206	    if event.event_type == TRIAL_READOUT:

   209	        if event.direction == DIR_MET:

   343	def _emit_hc_events(
   344	    news_items: list[NewsItem],
   345	    universe: Universe,
   346	    tracker: HaltTracker,
   347	    log_path: Optional[Path],

   349	    stats: RunStats,

   351	    slack_webhook_url: Optional[str] = None,
   352	) -> None:
   353	    """Detect + emit HC event-wire alerts (FDA actions + clinical readouts).
   354	
   355	    v1 CONSUMES the PR-wire NewsItems already fetched this poll: classify each,
   356	    and — for items whose issuer-embedded tickers are in the covered universe —
   357	    post an SA-faithful alert (design of record §4).
   358	
   359	    Attribution is HIGH-confidence by construction: the ticker was written into
   360	    the issuer's own release and extracted by the news parsers, so there is no
   361	    name-matching / misattribution risk (the openFDA sponsor→ticker resolver is
   362	    deferred to session 2).
   363	
   364	    Dedup key: f"{news_id}|{symbol}|{event_type}" in tracker.hc_events_emitted —
   365	    one alert per (press release, covered ticker, event type), ever. Post-then-
   366	    mark, identical gating to _emit_followups:
   367	      off      → stdout emit is the delivery → mark
   368	      live     → mark only on a successful post
   369	      dry-run  → render only, never post, never mark (re-testable)
   370	    """
   371	    for item in news_items:
   372	        ev = classify(item)
   373	        if ev is None:
   374	            continue
   375	        # v1 universe filter = issuer-extracted tickers ∩ covered Universe.
   376	        # (session 2 adds the sponsor→ticker resolver fallback here.)
   377	        covered = [t for t in item.tickers if universe.get(t)]
   378	        if not covered:
   379	            continue
   380	        for symbol in covered:
   381	            dedup_key = f"{item.news_id}|{symbol}|{ev.event_type}"
   382	            if dedup_key in tracker.hc_events_emitted:
   383	                continue  # one alert per (PR, ticker, type), ever
   384	            meta = universe.get(symbol)

   387	            rendered = render_hc_event(

   389	                subsector=(meta.subsector if meta else ""),
   390	            )

   393	            print(rendered)
   394	            print("=" * 60)
   395	            sys.stdout.flush()

   397	            # dry-run: render only — never post, never mark (re-testable).

   428	def _append_hc_log(log_path: Path, event, meta) -> None:
   429	    """Structured JSONL audit record for a delivered HC event (mirrors
   430	    _append_log; HCEvent has a different shape than HaltEvent so it gets its

   445	        "confidence": event.confidence,
   446	        "sector": meta.sector if meta else None,
   447	        "subsector": meta.subsector if meta else None,
   448	    }
   449	    with log_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
   450	        f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

     1	"""Precision-biased keyword classifier: NewsItem -> Optional[HCEvent].
     2	
     3	Design of record: research/sa_monitor_hc_event_wire_design_2026-07-13.md §3.
     4	Rules are anchored to the LOCKED real headlines in template-library.md
     5	§15 (Phase-N primary MISSED), §16 (FDA approval), §19 (Phase-N primary MET).
     6	
     7	Philosophy — "miss over misfire" (design §7): a wrong "Phase 3 met" or "FDA
     8	approval" alert costs more trust than a missed one. Every rule requires the
     9	headline to CLEARLY match a completed action. Ambiguous, future-tense,
    10	procedural, or presentation headlines return None.
    11	
    12	classify() only proposes an event *type/direction/phase*; it never decides
    13	whether to fire. The universe filter + attribution + dedup live in
    14	halt_monitor._emit_hc_events. classify() returns an HCEvent with symbol="".
    15	"""
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	import re
    19	from typing import Optional
    20	
    21	from ..news.types import NewsItem
    22	from .types import (
    23	    CLEARANCE,
    24	    CRL,
    25	    DIR_MET,
    26	    DIR_MISSED,
    27	    DIR_NA,
    28	    FDA_APPROVAL,
    29	    TRIAL_READOUT,
    30	    HCEvent,
    31	)
    32	
    33	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34	# Trial readout
    35	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36	
    37	# "Phase 1/2/3" (also "Phase 2B", "Phase 1/2") — capture the leading digit.
    38	_PHASE = re.compile(r"\bphase\s*([123])", re.I)
    39	
    40	# Result attached to the PRIMARY (or co-/dual primary) endpoint. Tying the
    41	# verb to "primary" (rather than matching "met"/"missed" anywhere) keeps the
    42	# reported DIRECTION faithful, which is the harmful failure mode to avoid.
    43	_MET_PRIMARY = re.compile(
    44	    r"\b(met|meets|achieved|achieves|hit|reached)\b"
    45	    r"(?:\s+(?:its|the|all|both|all\s+of\s+its))?"
    46	    r"(?:\s+(?:co-|dual|two|three|multiple))?"
    47	    r"\s+(?:co-)?primary\b",
    48	    re.I,
    49	)
    50	# Anchored to primary/endpoint so "did not meet enrollment target / guidance"
    51	# (with a stray Phase in the title) can't read as a failed readout.
    52	_MISSED_PRIMARY = re.compile(
    53	    r"\b(did\s+not\s+meet|does\s+not\s+meet|failed\s+to\s+meet|fails\s+to\s+meet"
    54	    r"|missed|misses|did\s+not\s+achieve|does\s+not\s+achieve)\b"
    55	    r"(?:\s+(?:its|the|both|all|all\s+of\s+its))?"
    56	    r"(?:\s+(?:co-|dual))?"
    57	    r"\s+(?:primary|co-primary|endpoint)",
    58	    re.I,
    59	)
    60	# A topline-data announcement with no explicit met/missed in the headline is
    61	# still a genuine readout crossing the wire — fired at direction n/a.
    62	_TOPLINE = re.compile(r"\btop-?line\b", re.I)
    63	
    64	# Future / procedural / presentation headlines that mention a Phase but are NOT
    65	# a readout. If ANY of these match, the trial-readout path returns None. This
    66	# is the primary defense for the negative test set.
    67	_TRIAL_NEGATIVE = re.compile(
    68	    r"\bpresent"          # present / presents / presented / presentation / presenting
    69	    r"|\bto\s+report\b|\bwill\s+report\b"
    70	    r"|\bto\s+announce\b|\bto\s+host\b|\bto\s+provide\b|\bto\s+release\b"
    71	    r"|\bto\s+webcast\b|\bwebcast\b|\bconference\s+call\b|\binvestor\s+day\b"
    72	    r"|\bposter\b|\bat\s+the\s+\w+.*(?:conference|congress|meeting|symposium)"
    73	    r"|\binitiat"          # initiate / initiation / initiates
    74	    r"|\bcommenc"          # commence / commences / commencement
    75	    r"|\bfirst[\s-]patient\b|\bdosing\s+of\b|\bdoses\s+(?:the\s+)?first\b"
    76	    r"|\bfirst\s+patient\s+dosed\b"
    77	    r"|\benroll"           # enroll / enrolls / enrolled / enrollment
    78	    r"|\btrial\s+design\b|\bstudy\s+design\b"
    79	    r"|\bto\s+begin\b|\bplans?\s+to\b|\bexpects?\s+to\b|\banticipat"
    80	    r"|\bon\s+track\b|\bdesign(?:s|ed)?\s+to\s+evaluate\b"
    81	    r"|\bcompletes?\s+enrollment\b",
    82	    re.I,
    83	)
    84	
    85	
    86	def _classify_trial(title: str, item: NewsItem) -> Optional[HCEvent]:
    87	    m_phase = _PHASE.search(title)
    88	    if not m_phase:
    89	        return None
    90	    if _TRIAL_NEGATIVE.search(title):
    91	        return None  # future / procedural / presentation → not a readout
    92	
    93	    missed = _MISSED_PRIMARY.search(title)
    94	    met = _MET_PRIMARY.search(title)
    95	    topline = _TOPLINE.search(title)
    96	
    97	    if missed:
    98	        direction = DIR_MISSED
    99	    elif met:
   100	        direction = DIR_MET
   101	    elif topline:
   102	        direction = DIR_NA
   103	    else:
   104	        return None  # Phase present but no result grammar → not a readout
   105	
   106	    return _build(item, TRIAL_READOUT, direction, phase=m_phase.group(1))
   107	
   108	
   109	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   110	# FDA regulatory actions
   111	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   112	
   113	# Action-verb-anchored approval grammar. Deliberately does NOT match bare
   114	# "FDA approval" so pending/procedural headlines ("PDUFA date for FDA approval
   115	# decision", "seeking FDA approval") don't fire.
   116	_APPROVAL = re.compile(
   117	    r"\bfda\s+approves\b"
   118	    r"|\bfda\s+approved\b"
   119	    r"|\bfda\s+approval\s+of\b"
   120	    r"|\breceive[sd]?\s+fda\s+approval\b"
   121	    r"|\bgranted\s+fda\s+approval\b"
   122	    r"|\bwins?\s+fda\s+approval\b"
   123	    r"|\bsecure[sd]?\s+fda\s+approval\b"
   124	    r"|\bgains?\s+fda\s+approval\b"
   125	    r"|\bfda\s+grants?\s+(?:full\s+|accelerated\s+|traditional\s+|marketing\s+)?approval\b"
   126	    r"|\bfda\s+has\s+granted\s+approval\b"
   127	    r"|\bapproved\s+by\s+the\s+fda\b",
   128	    re.I,
   129	)
   130	# Pending / procedural regulatory milestones that are NOT an approval. Any hit
   131	# suppresses the approval path (belt-and-suspenders atop the verb-anchored regex).
   132	_APPROVAL_NEGATIVE = re.compile(
   133	    r"\baccept(?:s|ed)?\b|\bpriority\s+review\b|\bfast\s+track\b"
   134	    r"|\bbreakthrough\s+therapy\b|\borphan\s+drug\b|\brare\s+pediatric\b"
   135	    r"|\badvisory\s+committee\b|\badcomm\b|\bpdufa\b"
   136	    r"|\bresubmi|\bseeking\b|\bawait|\bapplication\s+for\b"
   137	    r"|\bfiles?\s+for\b|\bsubmit|\bsubmission\b|\bto\s+request\b"
   138	    r"|\bcomplete\s+response\s+letter\b|\bpre-?market\s+notification\b",
   139	    re.I,
   140	)
   141	
   142	_CRL = re.compile(r"\bcomplete\s+response\s+letter\b|\bcrl\b", re.I)
   143	
   144	# Medtech clearance. Requires a completed clearance action — bare adjective
   145	# "FDA-cleared" (e.g. "operates an FDA-cleared facility") must NOT match.
   146	_CLEARANCE = re.compile(
   147	    r"\bfda\s+clears\b"
   148	    r"|\bfda\s+clearance\b"
   149	    r"|\breceive[sd]?\s+fda\s+clearance\b"
   150	    r"|\b510\(k\)\s+clearance\b"
   151	    r"|\bgranted\s+510\(k\)\b"
   152	    r"|\bde\s+novo\s+(?:clearance|authorization|grant)\b"
   153	    r"|\bpma\s+approval\b"
   154	    r"|\bfda\s+grants?\s+(?:de\s+novo|510)\b",
   155	    re.I,
   156	)
   157	
   158	
   159	def _classify_regulatory(title: str, item: NewsItem) -> Optional[HCEvent]:
   160	    # CRL first — a Complete Response Letter is unambiguous and distinct from
   161	    # an approval; it must never be mis-read as one.
   162	    if _CRL.search(title):
   163	        return _build(item, CRL, DIR_NA)
   164	    if _APPROVAL.search(title) and not _APPROVAL_NEGATIVE.search(title):
   165	        return _build(item, FDA_APPROVAL, DIR_NA)
   166	    if _CLEARANCE.search(title):
   167	        return _build(item, CLEARANCE, DIR_NA)
   168	    return None
   169	
   170	
   171	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   172	# Public entry point
   173	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   174	
   175	
   176	def _build(item: NewsItem, event_type: str, direction: str,
   177	           *, phase: str = "") -> HCEvent:
   178	    return HCEvent(
   179	        news_id=item.news_id,
   180	        symbol="",  # attribution decided by the emit path (tickers ∩ universe)
   181	        event_type=event_type,
   182	        direction=direction,
   183	        headline=item.title,
   184	        source=item.source,
   185	        url=item.url,
   186	        published_at=item.published_at,
   187	        phase=phase,
   188	        confidence="high",
   189	        raw_industries=tuple(item.industries),
   190	    )
   191	
   192	
   193	def classify(item: NewsItem) -> Optional[HCEvent]:
   194	    """Classify a NewsItem into an HCEvent, or None if it isn't one.
   195	
   196	    Precision-biased: returns None on any ambiguity. Regulatory actions
   197	    (approval / CRL / clearance) are checked before trial readouts — an
   198	    approval PR carries no Phase grammar, so the two families don't collide.
   199	    """
   200	    title = item.title or ""
   201	    if not title.strip():
   202	        return None
   203	
   204	    reg = _classify_regulatory(title, item)
   205	    if reg is not None:
   206	        return reg
   207	
   208	    return _classify_trial(title, item)

codex
The classifier has the suspected asymmetry: `_APPROVAL` is suppressed by pending/procedural negatives, but `_CLEARANCE` is not. I’m going to probe the exact attack headlines plus nearby variants so the findings are concrete.

     1	"""Render halt and resume events to the sa-monitor Slack/stdout template.
     2	
     3	Template grammar lives in template-library.md §3 (basic halt) and §6 (resume).
     4	We use a tightened sa-monitor variant — see those sections — that adds the
     5	exchange-feed reason code (which SA does not surface) and the elapsed-halt
     6	duration on resume (which we can compute from the feed timestamps).
     7	
     8	For Phase 1 D4: this module returns plain text strings for stdout printing.
     9	D5 will switch to Slack Block Kit per HEALTH_REPORTING.md §4.1 conventions.
    10	"""
    11	from __future__ import annotations
    12	
    13	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    14	from typing import Optional
    15	from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
    16	
    17	from .feeds.types import HaltEvent
    18	from .news.types import NewsItem
    19	
    20	_ET = ZoneInfo("America/New_York")
    21	
    22	# Presentation labels for news-wire sources. Mirrors enrichment._NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS
    23	# (kept local so template.py has no upward dependency on enrichment).
    24	_NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS = {
    25	    "prnewswire": "PR Newswire",
    26	    "businesswire": "Business Wire",
    27	    "globenewswire": "GlobeNewswire",
    28	}
    29	
    30	# Cap for the {headline} portion of a §7 Follow-up. SA follow-up subjects run
    31	# long; this keeps the Slack line readable while preserving the substance.
    32	_FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT = 200
    33	
    34	
    35	def _format_date_short(iso_date: str) -> str:
    36	    """ '2026-05-05' -> '5/05/26' (matches SA's body grammar). """
    37	    try:
    38	        dt = datetime.strptime(iso_date, "%Y-%m-%d")
    39	        return f"{dt.month}/{dt.day:02d}/{dt.year % 100:02d}"
    40	    except (ValueError, TypeError):
    41	        return iso_date
    42	
    43	
    44	def _format_time_hhmm(time_str: str) -> str:
    45	    """ '06:56:32' -> '06:56'. Resume notifications use HH:MM in SA's grammar. """
    46	    if not time_str:
    47	        return ""
    48	    parts = time_str.split(":")
    49	    if len(parts) >= 2:
    50	        return f"{parts[0]}:{parts[1]}"
    51	    return time_str
    52	
    53	
    54	def _format_price(price: Optional[float]) -> str:
    55	    """Format price as '$X.XX' or empty if None."""
    56	    if price is None:
    57	        return ""
    58	    return f"${price:.2f}"
    59	
    60	
    61	def _ticker_display_name(event: HaltEvent, name_override: str = "") -> str:
    62	    """Prefer name from sa_monitor_universe.json (override) over feed name."""
    63	    return (name_override or event.name or event.symbol).strip()
    64	
    65	
    66	def is_biotech(subsector: str) -> bool:
    67	    """True when the name's CM subsector is Biotech (case/space-insensitive)."""
    68	    return (subsector or "").strip().casefold() == "biotech"
    69	
    70	
    71	def biotech_triage_cta(symbol: str) -> str:
    72	    """Lean halt->triage hand-off nudge for a biotech halt (option A, 2026-06-16).
    73	
    74	    sa-monitor can't auto-invoke the Claude-driven biotech_triage, so instead of
    75	    pretending to trigger it we surface a copy-pasteable command. See the root
    76	    biotech_catalyst_architecture_plan.md (§3) for why this is human-mediated.
    77	    """
    78	    return f":dna: Biotech halt — triage this name?  ->  `triage {symbol}`"
    79	
    80	
    81	def render_halt(event: HaltEvent, *, sector: str = "", subsector: str = "",
    82	                name_override: str = "", note_context: Optional[str] = None) -> str:
    83	    """Render a halt notification — sa-monitor variant.
    84	
    85	    Phase 1 v0 template (per template-library.md §3):
    86	
    87	        SA: TICKER [Company halted, news pending]
    88	        HH:MM ET M/DD/YY [TICKER] halted at $X.XX, reason code [CODE - description]
    89	        Note TICKER is scheduled to report earnings this morning   <-- Phase 2 enrichment
    90	        Sector: {sector} / {subsector}
    91	    """
    92	    name = _ticker_display_name(event, name_override)
    93	    date_short = _format_date_short(event.halt_date)
    94	    time_hhmm = _format_time_hhmm(event.halt_time)
    95	    price_str = _format_price(event.last_price)
    96	
    97	    headline = f"SA: {event.symbol} [{name} halted, news pending]"
    98	
    99	    code_block = f"[{event.reason_code} - {event.reason_description}]"
   100	
   101	    if price_str:
   102	        body_line = (
   103	            f"{time_hhmm} ET {date_short} [{event.symbol}] halted at {price_str}, "
   104	            f"reason code {code_block}"
   105	        )
   106	    else:
   107	        body_line = (
   108	            f"{time_hhmm} ET {date_short} [{event.symbol}] halted, reason code "
   109	            f"{code_block}"
   110	        )
   111	
   112	    sector_line = ""
   113	    if sector:
   114	        sector_line = f"Sector: {sector}"
   115	        if subsector:
   116	            sector_line += f" / {subsector}"
   117	
   118	    parts = [headline, body_line]
   119	    if note_context:
   120	        parts.append(note_context)
   121	    if sector_line:
   122	        parts.append(sector_line)
   123	    if is_biotech(subsector):
   124	        parts.append(biotech_triage_cta(event.symbol))
   125	    parts.append(f"Source: {event.source} ({event.exchange})")
   126	    return "\n".join(parts)
   127	
   128	
   129	def _iso_utc_to_et(iso_ts: str) -> Optional[datetime]:
   130	    """ISO-8601 UTC timestamp -> ET datetime; None on malformed input."""
   131	    if not iso_ts:
   132	        return None
   133	    try:
   134	        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(iso_ts.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
   135	    except ValueError:
   136	        return None
   137	    if dt.tzinfo is None:
   138	        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   139	    return dt.astimezone(_ET)
   140	
   141	
   142	def followup_when(halt: HaltEvent, item: NewsItem) -> tuple[str, str]:
   143	    """(time_hhmm, date_short) for a §7 Follow-up header.
   144	
   145	    The follow-up crosses when the news breaks, so the header stamps the news
   146	    item's publish time in ET. Falls back to the halt's own timestamp if the
   147	    item has no parseable publish time. Shared by render_followup + Slack so
   148	    both render the identical header.
   149	    """
   150	    et = _iso_utc_to_et(item.published_at)
   151	    if et is not None:
   152	        return (f"{et.hour:02d}:{et.minute:02d}",
   153	                f"{et.month}/{et.day:02d}/{et.year % 100:02d}")
   154	    return (_format_time_hhmm(halt.halt_time), _format_date_short(halt.halt_date))
   155	
   156	
   157	def followup_headline(item: NewsItem) -> str:
   158	    """The substantive headline for a §7 Follow-up, capped for readability."""
   159	    headline = (item.title or "").strip()
   160	    if len(headline) > _FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT:
   161	        headline = headline[:_FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"
   162	    return headline
   163	
   164	
   165	def followup_source_label(item: NewsItem) -> str:
   166	    """Pretty wire-source label (e.g. 'PR Newswire') for a §7 Follow-up."""
   167	    return _NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS.get(item.source, item.source)
   168	
   169	
   170	def render_followup(halt: HaltEvent, item: NewsItem, *, sector: str = "",
   171	                    subsector: str = "", last_price: Optional[float] = None) -> str:
   172	    """Render a §7 Follow-up alert — the substantive news that broke on a
   173	    previously-halted covered name (template-library.md §7, webhook variant).
   174	
   175	        HH:MM ET M/DD/YY [StreetAccount] TICKER Follow-up: {headline} ($X.XX)
   176	        Follows the HH:MM ET halt on TICKER
   177	        Sector: {sector} / {subsector}
   178	        Source: {Wire} press release
   179	
   180	    sa-monitor posts via webhook (no Slack threading), so the original halt is
   181	    referenced in-body rather than via SA's archive block. Price is the halt's
   182	    last_price unless a fresher one is supplied.
   183	    """
   184	    symbol = halt.symbol
   185	    time_hhmm, date_short = followup_when(halt, item)
   186	    headline = followup_headline(item)
   187	
   188	    price = last_price if last_price is not None else halt.last_price
   189	    price_str = _format_price(price)
   190	
   191	    header = (
   192	        f"{time_hhmm} ET {date_short} [StreetAccount] {symbol} "
   193	        f"Follow-up: {headline}"
   194	    )
   195	    if price_str:
   196	        header += f" ({price_str})"
   197	
   198	    halt_hhmm = _format_time_hhmm(halt.halt_time)
   199	    back_ref = f"Follows the {halt_hhmm} ET halt on {symbol}"
   200	
   201	    parts = [header, back_ref]
   202	    if sector:
   203	        sector_line = f"Sector: {sector}"
   204	        if subsector:
   205	            sector_line += f" / {subsector}"
   206	        parts.append(sector_line)
   207	    parts.append(f"Source: {followup_source_label(item)} press release")
   208	    return "\n".join(parts)

   211	def hc_event_when(published_at: str) -> tuple[str, str]:
   212	    """(time_hhmm, date_short) in ET for an HC event, from its PR publish time.
   213	
   214	    Falls back to ("", "") if the timestamp is missing/malformed — the caller
   215	    then simply omits the stamp rather than printing garbage.
   216	    """
   217	    et = _iso_utc_to_et(published_at)
   218	    if et is None:
   219	        return ("", "")

   221	            f"{et.month}/{et.day:02d}/{et.year % 100:02d}")

   224	def hc_event_headline(headline: str) -> str:

   226	
   227	    SA reproduces issuer language; v1 delivers the issuer's own title (the
   228	    fact-dense §15/§19 efficacy body is a deferred LLM enhancement).
   229	    """
   230	    headline = (headline or "").strip()
   231	    if len(headline) > _FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT:
   232	        headline = headline[:_FOLLOWUP_HEADLINE_LIMIT].rsplit(" ", 1)[0] + "…"
   233	    return headline

   236	def render_hc_event(event, *, sector: str = "", subsector: str = "") -> str:
   237	    """Render an HC event-wire alert to plain text (stdout / --slack off).
   238	
   239	    event is an events.types.HCEvent. Mirrors render_followup's shape:

   242	        Sector: {sector} / {subsector}
   243	        [biotech triage CTA if biotech]
   244	        Source: {Wire} press release · {url}
   245	    """
   246	    time_hhmm, date_short = hc_event_when(event.published_at)
   247	    headline = hc_event_headline(event.headline)
   248	
   249	    when = f"{time_hhmm} ET {date_short} ".lstrip() if time_hhmm else ""

   251	
   252	    parts = [header]
   253	    if sector:
   254	        sector_line = f"Sector: {sector}"
   255	        if subsector:
   256	            sector_line += f" / {subsector}"
   257	        parts.append(sector_line)
   258	    if is_biotech(subsector):
   259	        parts.append(biotech_triage_cta(event.symbol))
   260	    label = _NEWS_SOURCE_LABELS.get(event.source, event.source)
   261	    source_line = f"Source: {label} press release"
   262	    if event.url:
   263	        source_line += f" · {event.url}"
   264	    parts.append(source_line)
   265	    return "\n".join(parts)
   266

   277	
   278	    Halt duration is omitted in v0 — it's computed in D6 with full timestamp
   279	    parsing once we know how the exchange feeds publish resume timestamps.
   280	    """

     1	"""Shared types for news-wire parsers."""
     2	from __future__ import annotations
     3	
     4	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     5	from typing import Optional
     6	
     7	
     8	@dataclass(frozen=True)
     9	class NewsItem:
    10	    """One news-wire item — same shape regardless of source.
    11	
    12	    Dedup key: `url` (the RSS guid is typically the URL itself).
    13	    """
    14	
    15	    source: str            # 'prnewswire' | 'businesswire' | 'globenewswire'
    16	    title: str
    17	    body: str              # plain text, HTML stripped, entities decoded
    18	    url: str
    19	    published_at: str      # ISO 8601 UTC, e.g. '2026-05-08T00:30:00+00:00'
    20	    tickers: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # extracted from body via regex
    21	    industries: tuple[str, ...] = ()  # source-specific taxonomy tags
    22	    raw: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    23	
    24	    @property
    25	    def news_id(self) -> str:
    26	        """Stable dedup key — same item across re-fetches resolves to one ID."""
    27	        return self.url
    28	
    29	    def __str__(self) -> str:
    30	        tickers_str = f" tickers={','.join(self.tickers)}" if self.tickers else ""
    31	        return f"{self.source}: {self.title[:80]}{tickers_str} ({self.published_at})"

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Halt-event deduplication.
     2	
     3	Both the Nasdaq RSS and NYSE CSV feeds report the same halt event (since the
     4	exchange tape consolidates both). Polling at 5s means each event re-appears
     5	in N consecutive polls until the feed rolls it off. We dedupe by halt-id
     6	`(symbol, halt_date, halt_time)` so the runner only fires one Slack message
     7	per real event, and one resume message per resume.
     8	
     9	State lives in memory for the duration of a single market-session run. A
    10	small persistence layer (writing seen-ids to disk between polls) is deferred
    11	to D6 — the end-to-end runner — so the script survives restart-mid-session.
    12	"""
    13	from __future__ import annotations
    14	
    15	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    16	from typing import Iterator
    17	
    18	from .feeds.types import HaltEvent
    19	
    20	# halt_id -> the most-recently-seen HaltEvent for that id (so resume info
    21	# accumulates as the feed publishes it).
    22	HaltId = tuple[str, str, str]
    23	
    24	
    25	@dataclass
    26	class HaltTracker:
    27	    """Tracks halt + resume events across feed polls.
    28	
    29	    Use:
    30	        tracker = HaltTracker()
    31	        for event in tracker.ingest(events_from_this_poll):
    32	            ... # only new halts and newly-published resumes come out
    33	    """
    34	
    35	    seen_halts: dict[HaltId, HaltEvent] = field(default_factory=dict)
    36	    resumes_emitted: set[HaltId] = field(default_factory=set)
    37	    # halt_ids we actually DELIVERED a halt alert for (passed the non-emit-code
    38	    # filter AND — in live mode — posted successfully). seen_halts records mere
    39	    # OBSERVATIONS; this records deliveries. A §7 Follow-up may only fire for a
    40	    # halt we delivered. Persisted so a restart preserves the gate.
    41	    emitted_halts: set[HaltId] = field(default_factory=set)
    42	    # halt_ids for which a §7 Follow-up alert has already fired — one per halt,
    43	    # ever. Persisted alongside seen_halts so a mid-session restart never
    44	    # re-emits a follow-up.
    45	    followed_up: set[HaltId] = field(default_factory=set)
    46	    # Delivery-dedup for the HC event-wire lane. Keys are strings of the form
    47	    # f"{news_id}|{symbol}|{event_type}" — one alert per (press release,
    48	    # covered ticker, event type), ever. Persisted like followed_up so a
    49	    # mid-session restart never re-emits an HC event. (Mirrors emitted_halts /
    50	    # followed_up exactly; unlike them, keyed by string not HaltId.)
    51	    hc_events_emitted: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)
    52	
    53	    def ingest(self, events: list[HaltEvent]) -> Iterator[tuple[str, HaltEvent]]:
    54	        """Diff `events` against state and yield (kind, event) for new ones.
    55	
    56	        kind ∈ {"halt", "resume"}.
    57	
    58	        - halt: first time we see this halt_id
    59	        - resume: previously-seen halt now has a resume_trade_time populated
    60	          AND we haven't emitted a resume for this halt_id yet
    61	        """
    62	        for event in events:
    63	            hid = event.halt_id
    64	            previous = self.seen_halts.get(hid)
    65	
    66	            if previous is None:
    67	                # First sighting — emit halt
    68	                self.seen_halts[hid] = event
    69	                yield ("halt", event)
    70	                # If the very first sighting already includes a resume time
    71	                # (rare — usually catch-up from a feed roll), also emit resume
    72	                if event.is_resumed and hid not in self.resumes_emitted:
    73	                    self.resumes_emitted.add(hid)
    74	                    yield ("resume", event)
    75	                continue
    76	
    77	            # Already seen — update stored event (resume info may have
    78	            # populated since the prior poll) and check whether resume
    79	            # is freshly-known.
    80	            self.seen_halts[hid] = event
    81	            if event.is_resumed and hid not in self.resumes_emitted:
    82	                self.resumes_emitted.add(hid)
    83	                yield ("resume", event)
    84	
    85	    def __len__(self) -> int:
    86	        return len(self.seen_halts)
    87	
    88	    def reset(self) -> None:
    89	        """Clear state — used at session boundary or for tests."""
    90	        self.seen_halts.clear()

from src.events.classify import classify

codex
The direct probes confirmed two definite false-positive classes: pending clearance headlines and “topline … expected in …” future-data headlines. Approval/commentary references also fire because `FDA approval of` is treated as completed action with no subject/issuer guard.
codex
I’m also checking the ticker extraction assumption because attribution quality depends on what `item.tickers` means in the parsers, not just on `_emit_hc_events`. If it extracts mentioned exchange/ticker pairs beyond the issuer, competitor/commentary PRs become more dangerous.

     1	"""Shared utilities for news-wire RSS parsers.
     2	
     3	Three concerns shared across PRN/BW/GNW:
     4	- HTML→plain-text body cleaning (RSS descriptions are HTML/CDATA)
     5	- RFC-822 date parsing (all three publish 'Fri, 8 May 2026 00:30:00 +0000')
     6	- Ticker extraction from body text via exchange-prefix regex
     7	
     8	Adapter modules import these instead of duplicating per-source. If a source
     9	ever needs custom logic (e.g. PRN's industry tags vs BW's enclosures), keep
    10	the source-specific bits in that module.
    11	"""
    12	from __future__ import annotations
    13	
    14	import html
    15	import re
    16	from datetime import datetime, timezone
    17	from email.utils import parsedate_to_datetime
    18	from typing import Optional
    19	
    20	
    21	# Match (NASDAQ: ABCD), (Nasdaq:ABCD), (NYSE: ABCD), (NYSE Arca: ABCD), etc.
    22	# Captures the ticker portion. Tickers include letters, digits, dots, dashes
    23	# (for share-class suffixes like BRK.A), capped at 8 chars to avoid matching
    24	# stray prose like "(NYSE: see story below)". Case-sensitive on the ticker so
    25	# we don't match lowercase prose.
    26	TICKER_RX = re.compile(
    27	    r"\((?:NASDAQ|Nasdaq|NYSE\s+American|NYSE\s+Arca|NYSE|TSX|TSXV|OTCQB|OTCQX|NEO|CSE)\s*:\s*"
    28	    r"([A-Z][A-Z0-9.\-]{0,7})\s*\)"
    29	)
    30	
    31	_TAG_RX = re.compile(r"<[^>]+>")
    32	_WS_RX = re.compile(r"\s+")
    33	
    34	
    35	def strip_html(s: str) -> str:
    36	    """Decode entities + remove tags + collapse whitespace.
    37	
    38	    RSS bodies are HTML wrapped in CDATA. We don't try to preserve structure;
    39	    matchers downstream want a flat plain-text blob.
    40	    """
    41	    if not s:
    42	        return ""
    43	    text = _TAG_RX.sub(" ", s)
    44	    text = html.unescape(text)
    45	    text = _WS_RX.sub(" ", text).strip()
    46	    return text
    47	
    48	
    49	def parse_pubdate(s: str) -> Optional[str]:
    50	    """RFC 822 -> ISO 8601 UTC.
    51	
    52	    Returns None on malformed input — the caller decides whether to skip the
    53	    item or fall back to fetch time.
    54	    """
    55	    if not s:
    56	        return None
    57	    try:
    58	        dt = parsedate_to_datetime(s.strip())
    59	    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    60	        return None
    61	    if dt is None:
    62	        return None
    63	    if dt.tzinfo is None:
    64	        # Treat naive timestamps as UTC — RSS feeds without tz are rare and
    65	        # localizing them blind would be worse than this assumption.
    66	        dt = dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    67	    else:
    68	        dt = dt.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    69	    return dt.isoformat(timespec="seconds")
    70	
    71	
    72	def extract_tickers(text: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    73	    """Find exchange-prefix ticker mentions in text.
    74	
    75	    Returns a deduped tuple, preserving first-seen order. Ignores tickers
    76	    longer than 8 chars (defensive against prose collisions).
    77	    """
    78	    if not text:
    79	        return ()
    80	    seen: list[str] = []
    81	    for m in TICKER_RX.finditer(text):
    82	        t = m.group(1).upper()
    83	        if t and t not in seen:
    84	            seen.append(t)
    85	    return tuple(seen)
    86	
    87	
    88	def strip_ns(tag: str) -> str:
    89	    """'{http://...}name' -> 'name' — for namespaced RSS elements."""
    90	    return tag.rsplit("}", 1)[-1] if "}" in tag else tag

codex
**Findings**

- High: pending/negative clearance headlines false-fire as completed clearance. `_CLEARANCE` matches bare `FDA clearance` / `510(k) clearance`, and `_classify_regulatory` applies no negative gate before returning `CLEARANCE`.  
  [src/events/classify.py:146](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:146>), [src/events/classify.py:166](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:166>)  
  Repro: `classify("Acme submits 510(k) for FDA clearance...") -> clearance`; also `did not receive FDA clearance`, `fails to receive FDA clearance`, `FDA denies 510(k) clearance`, `withdraws 510(k) clearance application` all classify as `clearance`.

- High: negative approval direction can be inverted. `_APPROVAL` matches `receive FDA approval` / `win FDA approval`, while `_APPROVAL_NEGATIVE` does not include explicit denial grammar like `did not`, `fails to`, `not receive`.  
  [src/events/classify.py:120](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:120>), [src/events/classify.py:132](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:132>)  
  Repro: `classify("Acme did not receive FDA approval for acmezumab") -> fda_approval`; `classify("Acme fails to win FDA approval for acmezumab") -> fda_approval`.

- High: competitor/commentary approval references fire. `FDA approval of` is treated as an approval event with no guard for `comments on`, `congratulates`, `statement regarding`, etc.; `_emit_hc_events` then attributes to every covered ticker in `item.tickers`.  
  [src/events/classify.py:119](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:119>), [src/halt_monitor.py:377](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/halt_monitor.py:377>), [src/news/parsers.py:72](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/news/parsers.py:72>)  
  Repro: `classify("Acme comments on FDA approval of RivalDrug for advanced disease") -> fda_approval`; with `tickers=("ACME",)` and ACME covered, `_emit_hc_events` posts it.

- High: future topline trial headlines false-fire when phrased as “expected in.” `_TRIAL_NEGATIVE` catches `expects to` but not adjectival `expected in`, so `_TOPLINE` wins.  
  [src/events/classify.py:62](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:62>), [src/events/classify.py:79](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:79>)  
  Repro: `classify("Acme announces topline Phase 3 data expected in Q4") -> trial_readout:n/a`. The specific `Phase 3 readout on track for 2H26` probe did not fire.

- High: bare `CRL` collides with company abbreviations/tickers and fires as Complete Response Letter.  
  [src/events/classify.py:142](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:142>)

**Findings**

- High: pending/negative clearance headlines false-fire as completed clearance. `_CLEARANCE` matches bare `FDA clearance` / `510(k) clearance`, and `_classify_regulatory` applies no negative gate before returning `CLEARANCE`.  
  [src/events/classify.py:146](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:146>), [src/events/classify.py:166](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:166>)  
  Repro: `classify("Acme submits 510(k) for FDA clearance...") -> clearance`; also `did not receive FDA clearance`, `fails to receive FDA clearance`, `FDA denies 510(k) clearance`, `withdraws 510(k) clearance application` all classify as `clearance`.

- High: negative approval direction can be inverted. `_APPROVAL` matches `receive FDA approval` / `win FDA approval`, while `_APPROVAL_NEGATIVE` does not include explicit denial grammar like `did not`, `fails to`, `not receive`.  
  [src/events/classify.py:120](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:120>), [src/events/classify.py:132](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:132>)  
  Repro: `classify("Acme did not receive FDA approval for acmezumab") -> fda_approval`; `classify("Acme fails to win FDA approval for acmezumab") -> fda_approval`.

- High: competitor/commentary approval references fire. `FDA approval of` is treated as an approval event with no guard for `comments on`, `congratulates`, `statement regarding`, etc.; `_emit_hc_events` then attributes to every covered ticker in `item.tickers`.  
  [src/events/classify.py:119](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:119>), [src/halt_monitor.py:377](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/halt_monitor.py:377>), [src/news/parsers.py:72](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/news/parsers.py:72>)  
  Repro: `classify("Acme comments on FDA approval of RivalDrug for advanced disease") -> fda_approval`; with `tickers=("ACME",)` and ACME covered, `_emit_hc_events` posts it.

- High: future topline trial headlines false-fire when phrased as “expected in.” `_TRIAL_NEGATIVE` catches `expects to` but not adjectival `expected in`, so `_TOPLINE` wins.  
  [src/events/classify.py:62](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:62>), [src/events/classify.py:79](</mnt/c/Users/jroyp/Dropbox/Claude Folder/sa-monitor/src/events/classify.py:79>)  
  Repro: `classify("Acme announces topline Phase 3 data expected in Q4") -> trial_readout:n/a`. The specific `Phase 3 readout on track for 2H26` probe did not fire.
